## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 92 page(s) - 264 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R73: Text spacing can be adjusted without loss of content](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73)
**Issue**: The line height of the paragraph is less than 1.5

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<p class="intro sm">...</p>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/p[@class="intro sm"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/p[@class="intro sm"]')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://cityofkingston.ca/

#### Rule: [SIA-R11: Button elements have an accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r11)
**WCAG**: [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: The link does not have an accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="/" title="">...</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.8](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/target-size-minimum) (Level AA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<button class="btn base-btn-bg base-btn-bg base-btn-bg-solid base-btn-bg-hover-solid base-btn-text base-btn-borders accept-cookies" aria-label="Agree to cookie consent message">...</button>`
- XPath: `/button[@class="btn base-btn-bg base-btn-bg base-btn-bg-solid base-btn-bg-hover-solid base-btn-text base-btn-borders accept-cookies"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: The font size is specified using an absolute unit
- HTML: `<p class="heading  footer-heading ">City of Kingston</p>`
- XPath: `/p[@class="heading  footer-heading "]`

- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: Elements must only use supported ARIA attributes
- HTML: `<p aria-level="3">Kingston is Canada’s museum capital. We have something for everyone with 24 museums and historic sites and three art galleries. This includes City-owned museums like PumpHouse and MacLachlan Woodworking Museum, along with Kingston City Hall National Historic Site.</p>`
- XPath: `p[aria-level="3"]:nth-child(1)`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: Elements must only use supported ARIA attributes
- HTML: `<p aria-level="3"><a href="http://www.kingstonmuseums.ca/">The Kingston and Area Association of Museums, Galleries and Historic Sites</a> is a not-for-profit group that supports our cultural heritage sector. Visit their website to learn about museums to explore in Kingston.</p>`
- XPath: `p[aria-level="3"]:nth-child(2)`

#### Rule: [meta-viewport-large](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/meta-viewport-large?application=playwright)
**Impact**: minor

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: Users should be able to zoom and scale the text up to 500%
- HTML: `<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, shrink-to-fit=no, maximum-scale=2">`
- XPath: `meta[name="viewport"]`

#### Rule: [role-img-alt](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/role-img-alt?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**WCAG**: [SC 1.1.1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/non-text-content) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-04-17
- Message: [role="img"] elements must have alternative text
- HTML: `<div class="image background-ima..." style="background-image: ur..." data-bgset="/media/uxtcvkef/bann..." role="img" aria-label="">`
- XPath: `.background-image`
